Need compute C matrix function:
```
def compute_coeff_matrix(h):
    n=len(h); M=2*n-1; C=np.zeros((n,M), dtype=float)
    for r in range(n):
       cr=C[r]
       cr[0]=1-2*h[r]
       left_len=r
       if left_len:
          left=h[r-1::-1]
          cr[idx_left[r]] += -left  # P
          cr[idx_left[r]] += (1-left) # Q? Wait same idx for left? For left d, idxP=2d-1, idxQ=2d both use same d but different indices. Need separate idx arrays.
       right_len=n-r-1
       right=h[r+1:]
       cr[idxRightP[r]] += (1-right)
       cr[idxRightQ[r]] += -right
    return C
```
Need precomputed `idxP_left[r] = 2*np.arange(1,r+1)-1`, `idxQ_left[r]=2*np.arange(1,r+1)`, `idxP_right[r]=2*np.arange(1,n-r)-1`, `idxQ_right=2*np.arange(1,n-r)`.
Then:
left_rev = h[r-1::-1]  # d=1..r
cr[idxP[r]] -= left_rev
cr[idxQ[r]] += 1 - left_rev
right = h[r+1:]

Function evaluate(t): tmp = corr + L*t (out); tmp[0] += q0*t2; tmp[idxP] += t2; tmp[idxQ] += t2; return tmp.max(). Note base corr includes old corr. L*C[i]-C[j] derivative. Batch Best-Pair Search:
- Given current h, corr, C.
- Select a set of candidate pairs (random or structured). For each, run line search (40 itin) to find min value (max count) and t.
- Choose pair with best (lowest value) if less than current_max - tol.
- Apply best pair, update h, corr, recompute C (or maybe update C? recompute).
- Repeat until no improvement in batch or time.

Batch selection function:

h = project(h - eta * grad)

if improves accept else reduce eta.
```

`project_feasible(h,n)` can be a few loops; if constructing many, use fast:
```
h = np.clip(h,0,1)
for _ in range(20):
  diff = target - h.sum()
  if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
  if diff>0:
    room = 1-h; idx = np.argmax(room)
    if room[idx] <=0: break
    h[idx] = min(1, h[idx]+diff)
  else:
    idx=np.argmax(h)
    if h[idx]<=0: break
    h[idx] = max(0, h[idx]+diff) # diff negative
return h
```
Adjusting one coordinate can push it out of bounds if diff large; but diff small. For random/bias use weighted. For general projection, use iterative distribution.

```
def cosine_h(n, f, amp, phase):
    i=np.arange(n)
    theta=2*np.pi*f*i/n + phase
    h=0.5 + amp*np.cos(theta)
    return make_feasible(h,n)
```

  Need exact target n/2. Use iterative proportional distribution but exact final:
```
def make_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(),0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(1000):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1-h
            if room.sum()<=1e-15: break
            h += (diff/room.sum())*room
        else:
            if h.sum()<=1e-15: break
            h += (diff/h.sum())*h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final exact adjust
    for _ in range(10):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        if diff>0:
            idx=int(np.argmax(1-h))
            h[idx]=min(1.0, h[idx]+diff)
        else:
            idx=int(np.argmax(h))
            h[idx]=max(0.0, h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```
This may not achieve exact if diff too large for one coordinate, but repeated. It distributes globally first; fine. For small n and random. Use `np.clip` after.
- `evaluate_candidate(h)`: project and objective.

def cosine_candidate(n, freq, amp, phase):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    theta=2.0*np.pi*freq*i/n + phase
    h=0.5 + amp*np.cos(theta)
    return h  # not project yet? project for eval
```

`compute_corr_pq(h,n)`:
```
M=2*n-1
corr=np.empty(M)
corr[0]=np.dot(h,1-h)
for k in range(1,n):
    a=h[:-k]; b=h[k:]
    corr[2*k-1]=np.dot(a,1-b)
    corr[2*k]=np.dot(b,1-a)
return corr
```
This uses n-1 Python dot calls; for n=250, ~250 dots length average 125 -> okay. For n=400, 400 dots. In objective evals for smooth step, using this instead of np.correlate maybe slower due Python loop. For eval many amps, use `np.correlate` easier. But C/corr P/Q needs loops. 5. `make_index_maps(n)`:

```
def make_index_maps(n):
    Pl=[]; Ql=[]; Pr=[]; Qr=[]
    for r in range(n):
       l=r
       if l:
          arr=2*np.arange(1,l+1)-1
          Pl.append(arr); Ql.append(arr+1)
       else: Pl.append(np.empty(0,dtype=int)); Ql.append(...)
       rr=n-r-1
       if rr:
          arr=2*np.arange(1,rr+1)-1
          Pr.append(arr); Qr.append(arr+1)
       else...
    return Pl,Ql,Pr,Qr
```
Index dtype int.

6. `compute_coeff_matrix(h,n,maps)`:
```
Pl,Ql,Pr,Qr=maps
M=2*n-1
C=np.zeros((n,M), dtype=float)
for r in range(n):
    cr=C[r]
    cr[0]=1.0-2.0*h[r]
    if Pl[r].size:
       left=h[r-1::-1]
       cr[Pl[r]] -= left
       cr[Ql[r]] += 1.0-left
    if Pr[r].size:
       right=h[r+1:]
       cr[Pr[r]] += 1.0-right
       cr[Qr[r]] -= right
return C
```
Advanced indexing with duplicate indices? For Pl and Ql different; within a list unique; no duplicate additions? For a given r, right terms for P and Q different idx, left different. OK.
Could `cr[Pl[r]] += ...` with advanced indexing does buffered assignment; okay. If duplicate, would not accumulate but none.

10. `gradient_simple_step(h,n,temps,amplitudes,rng)`:
Top-level returns new h if improvement else None. Uses current best? We can do within function.
```
def gradient_simple_step(h, n, c_cur, deadline_frac? )
    corr=compute_corr_pq(h,n)
    C=compute_coeff_matrix(h,n,maps) # need maps passed
    maxc=corr.max()
    # generate directions from several temps
    best_h=None; best_c=c_cur
    for temp in temps:
       w=np.exp((corr-maxc)/temp); w/=w.sum()
       g=C.T @ w
       g-=g.mean()
       gm=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gm <1e-12: continue
       g /= gm
       for amp in amplitudes:
          h2=h - amp*g
          h2=np.clip(h2,0,1)
          h2=project_feasible(h2,n)
          if not np.allclose? no
          c2=c5_objective(h2,n)
          if c2 < best_c - 1e-12: best_c=c2; best_h=h2
    return best_h
```

We need maps passed. Could integrate in local optimizer.

Potential performance of gradient step: `project_feasible` loops up to 1000; for small diff okay. We can define a faster `project_feasible_fast` for smooth steps:
```
def quick_project(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 diff=0.5*n - h.sum()
 if abs(diff)<1e-13: return h
 for _ in range(20):
   if diff>0:
      idx=np.argmax(1-h); room=1-h[idx]; add=min(diff,room); h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
   else:
      idx=np.argmax(h); mass=h[idx]; sub=min(-diff,mass); h[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
 return h
```

Let's implement `project_feasible` robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(3):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-12: return h
        if diff>0:
            idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
            room=1.0-h[idx]
            if room <= 1e-15: break
            h[idx]+=diff if diff<room else room
        else:
            idx=int(np.argmax(h))
            if h[idx] <=1e-15: break
            h[idx]+=diff if diff> -h[idx] else -h[idx]
    # weighted
    for _ in range(1000): ...
    np.clip...
    for _ in range(3): ...
    return h
```
If diff large (e.g., random h), one-coordinate can clamp and not fix; weighted handles. Good.

Let's verify `compute_corr_pq` formula. For k in 1..n-1:
`a=h[:n-k]; b=h[k:]` length n-k.
P = dot(a, 1-b) = ∑ h_i(1-h_{i+k})
Q = dot(b, 1-a) = ∑ h_{i+k}(1-h_i)
At k=0, P=dot(h,1-h). Full np.correlate set includes these. Max same. Good.

Let's sketch full code:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def project_feasible(h,n): ...

Need no `lambda` for key. `generate_candidates` can use set and loops.

Let's write each function carefully.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    # quick
    for _ in range(5):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            return h
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
            if room <= 1e-15:
                break
            add = diff if diff < room else room
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            mass = h[idx]
            if mass <= 1e-15:
                break
            sub = -diff if -diff < mass else mass
            h[idx] -= sub
    # weighted
    for _ in range(1000):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    for _ in range(10):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
            if room <= 1e-15: break
            h[idx] += diff if diff < room else room
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            if h[idx] <= 1e-15: break
            h[idx] += diff if diff > -h[idx] else -h[idx]
    return h
```
If first quick already exact, returns. Good.

`c5_objective`:
```
def c5_objective(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)
```
If n=0? not.

`compute_corr_pq`:
```
def compute_corr_pq(h,n):
    M=2*n-1
    corr=np.empty(M,float)
    corr[0]=float(np.dot(h, 1.0-h))
    for k in range(1,n):
        m=n-k
        a=h[:m]
        b=h[k:k+m] # h[k:]
        corr[2*k-1]=float(np.dot(a, 1.0-b))
        corr[2*k]=float(np.dot(b, 1.0-a))
    return corr
```
Using `1.0-b` allocates; okay. Could `np.dot(a,1.0); -np.dot(a,b)` maybe faster. P = a.sum() - dot(a,b). Since P=∑a(1-b)=∑a -∑ab. Q=∑b -∑ab. So:
```
ab=np.dot(a,b); corr[2k-1]=a.sum()-ab; corr[2k]=b.sum()-ab
```
This avoids 1-b and extra subtract. Good. At k=0, corr0=sum h(1-h)=sumh - sumsq. Use.
```
s=sum(h); corr[0]=s - np.dot(h,h)
for k:
  a=h[:-k]; b=h[k:]; ab=np.dot(a,b); corr[2k-1]=a.sum()-ab; corr[2k]=b.sum()-ab
```
This also improves compute_C? C formulas derive from P=∑a-ab, Q=∑b-ab. C derivative for P wrt first a: 1 - h_v; wrt second b: -h_u. We used. Good.
Need for `compute_corr_pq`, sums of slices each time; can precompute? Not.

`make_index_maps`: For each r, left indices d=1..r: P idx=2d-1, Q idx=2d. Right same. Use dtype=np.intp.
```
def make_index_maps(n):
    Pl=[None]*n; Ql=[None]*n; Pr=[None]*n; Qr=[None]*n
    empty=np.empty(0,dtype=np.intp)
    for r in range(n):
       l=r
       if l>0:
          arr=2*np.arange(1,l+1,dtype=np.intp)-1
          Pl[r]=arr; Ql[r]=arr+1
       else: Pl[r]=empty; Ql[r]=empty
       rr=n-r-1
       if rr>0:
          arr=2*np.arange(1,rr+1,dtype=np.intp)-1
          Pr[r]=arr; Qr[r]=arr+1
       else: Pr[r]=empty; Qr[r]=empty
    return Pl,Ql,Pr,Qr
```
Shared empty okay.

`compute_coeff_matrix`:
```
def compute_coeff_matrix(h,n,maps):
    Pl,Ql,Pr,Qr=maps
    M=2*n-1
    C=np.zeros((n,M), dtype=float)
    for r in range(n):
       cr=C[r]
       cr[0]=1.0-2.0*h[r]
       idx=Pl[r]
       if idx.size:
          left=h[r-1::-1]
          cr[idx] -= left
          cr[Ql[r]] += 1.0-left
       idx=Pr[r]
       if idx.size:
          right=h[r+1:]
          cr[idx] += 1.0-right
          cr[Qr[r]] -= right
    return C
```
`h[r-1::-1]` returns view? It returns copy maybe; okay. Could use `left = h[:r][::-1]`. For r=0 skip.
Potential duplicate indices? For left, Pl indices d=1..r; no duplicates. Good.

`transfer_value` and `minimize_transfer` use `corr` as current count, `L` length M. For speed, `tmp` preallocated.
```
def transfer_value(corr, L, t, qP, qQ, tmp):
    np.multiply(L, t, out=tmp)
    tmp += corr
    t2=t*t
    tmp[0] -= 2.0*t2
    tmp[qP] += t2
    tmp[qQ] += t2
    return float(tmp.max())
```
If `t` is very small, okay. If qP or qQ negative? d>0.
`minimize_transfer`:
```
def minimize_transfer(corr,L,lo,hi,qP,qQ,tmp):
    if hi < lo:
       lo,hi=hi,lo
    span=hi-lo
    if span < 1e-14:
       v=transfer_value(...0); return 0,v
    best_t=0.0; best_v=transfer_value(...,0)
    # endpoints
    v=transfer_value(...,lo); if v<best_v: ...
    v=transfer_value(...,hi); ...
    NG=15
    if span > 1e-5:
       step=span/(NG+1)
       grid_t=[lo]
       grid_v=[vlo?] # we already eval lo
       for a in range(1,NG+2):
          t=lo+a*step
          v=transfer_value(...)
          grid_t.append(t); grid_v.append(v)
          if v<best_v: best...
       # bracket around grid best
       idx=int(np.argmin(grid_v))
       a=max(0, idx-1); b=min(len(grid_t)-1, idx+1)
       left=grid_t[a]; right=grid_t[b]
    else:
       left=lo; right=hi
    # ternary
    for _ in range(22):
       m1=left + (right-left)/3.0
       m2=right - (right-left)/3.0
       v1=transfer_value(...,m1)
       v2=transfer_value(...,m2)
       if v1 <= v2:
          right=m2
       else:
          left=m1
    t=0.5*(left+right)
    v=transfer_value(...,t)
    if v<best_v: best...
    # maybe evaluate endpoints of bracket
    for tt in (left,right): ...
    return best_t,best_v
```
We duplicate endpoint evals; okay. Need store `vlo` to avoid recalc? Not necessary.
Number eval: initial 3 + NG+1=16 + 22*2=44 +3 =66. Maybe high. Reduce NG=9 (10 grid), ternary 18: total 3+10+36+3=52. Good. Use NG=9? Grid step 1/10=0.1; bracket width 0.1; ternary 18 width 0.1*(2/3)^18=7e-5; good. Nonunimodal minima separated >0.1? Maybe unlikely. Use NG=12, ternary 20: 3+13+40+3=59. We'll use NG=12, ternary=18. Total ~55. For 100 candidates batch ->5500 evals. Good.
If span small, reduce ternary. Could `if span < 0.05: iterations=10` else 18. Nice.

`generate_candidates`:
```
def generate_candidates(n, h, C, corr, rng, max_candidates=160):
    cands=[]; seen=set()
    maxc=float(corr.max())
    top_k=min(10, 2*n-1)
    top_lags=np.argpartition(corr, -top_k)[-top_k:]
    # sort descending maybe
    top_lags=top_lags[np.argsort(corr[top_lags])[::-1]]
    # helper add inline
    # active lag extremes
    for s in top_lags:
        order=np.argsort(C[:,s])
        # cross extremes
        for idx_a in (n-1,n-2,n-3):
            for idx_b in (0,1,2):
                if idx_a>=n or idx_b>=n: continue
                i=int(order[idx_a]); j=int(order[idx_b])
                if i==j: continue
                if i>j: i,j=j,i
                key=i*n+j
                if key not in seen:
                    seen.add(key); cands.append((i,j))
        # random top/bottom
        for _ in range(2):
             ai=int(rng.randint(0, min(5,n)))
             bi=int(rng.randint(0, min(5,n)))
             i=int(order[-1-ai]); j=int(order[bi])
             if i==j: continue
             if i>j: i,j=j,i
             key=...
    # weighted active
    w=np.zeros(n,float)
    # weights softmax of top lag corr
    # compute wsum
    # Use exp((corr[s]-maxc)/1.0)
    for s in top_lags:
        w += C[:,s] * float(np.exp((corr[s]-maxc)/1.0))
    if np.max(np.abs(w)) > 0:
       wo=np.argsort(w)
       for a in range(3):
          for b in range(3):
              i=int(wo[-1-a]); j=int(wo[b])
              add...
    # h extremes
    hi_order=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:min(5,n)]
    lo_order=np.argsort(h)[:min(5,n)]
    for ai in range(len(hi_order)):
       for bi in range(len(lo_order)):
          add...
    # random until max
    attempts=0
    while len(cands)<max_candidates and attempts < 5*max_candidates:
       attempts+=1
       i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
       if i==j: continue
       if i>j: i,j=j,i
       key=...
       add
    # if still fewer than max, add near random? not necessary
    return cands[:max_candidates]
```
No closures. `key=i*n+j` with i<j. `n` up to 400, key int. In add, check if len(max_candidates)? We can skip adding if len >= max to save. But active may exceed; cap.
Random pairs duplicated? Use seen.

`gradient_step(h,n,maps,current_c)`:
```
def gradient_step(h,n,maps,current_c,temps=(0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0),amps=(0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05)):
    corr=compute_corr_pq(h,n)
    C=compute_coeff_matrix(h,n,maps)
    maxc=float(corr.max())
    best_h=None; best_c=current_c
    M=len(corr)
    for temp in temps:
        # w = exp((corr-maxc)/temp), may underflow okay
        diff=(corr-maxc)/temp
        w=np.exp(diff)
        tot=w.sum()
        if tot<=0: w=np.exp(1.0)*? no; use np.exp(diff-diff.max())
        # better:
        m=diff.max(); w=np.exp(diff-m); w/=w.sum()
        g=C.T @ w
        g-=float(g.mean())
        gm=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gm < 1e-12: continue
        g /= gm
        for amp in amps:
            h2=h - amp*g
            np.clip(h2,0,1,out=h2)
            h2=project_feasible(h2,n)
            # maybe if h2 too close to h skip
            c2=c5_objective(h2,n)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy()
    return best_h
```
O(temps*amps) evaluations. Could be 20. Use temps 3 amps 5=15. Good.
But `project_feasible` can change h2 significantly if amp large and clipping; okay.
We can speed by using `np.clip` and quick project. `project_feasible` okay.

`optimize_h(h,n,deadline,rng, light=False)`:
This is main local optimizer. Need maps, buffers.
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_h(h, n, deadline, rng, light=False):
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c5_objective(h,n)
    maps = make_index_maps(n)
    M = 2*n-1
    Lbuf = np.empty(M); tmp = np.empty(M)
    corr = compute_corr_pq(h,n); C = compute_coeff_matrix(h,n,maps)
    max_corr = float(corr.max())
    no_improve = 0
    batch_limit = 3 if not light else 1
    candidate_count = 160 if not light else 80
    gradient_temp = (0.5,1.0,2.0) if not light else (1.0,2.0)
    amp = (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02) if not light else (0.005,0.01)
    perturb_scale = 0.02
    perturb_count = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 3:
        # gradient attempt every few loops
        if (lights) and time<deadline-1:
            newh = gradient_step(h,n,maps,best_c? current_c, gradient_temp, amp)
            if newh is not None:
                h=newh; c=c5_objective(h,n); if c<best_c: update; recompute corr/C; no_improve=0; perturb_scale=...; continue
        # pair batches
        batch_improved=False
        for batch in range(batch_limit):
            if time.time() > deadline - 2: return best_h,best_c
            cands=generate_candidates(n,h,C,corr,rng,candidate_count)
            if not cands: break
            best_val = max_corr + 1e-12
            best_pair=None; best_t=0.0
            for (i,j) in cands:
                if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
                lo=-float(h[i]); hi=1.0-float(h[j])
                if hi-lo < 1e-12: continue
                d=abs(i-j)
                qP=2*d-1; qQ=qP+1
                np.subtract(C[i], C[j], out=Lbuf)
                t,v = minimize_transfer(corr,Lbuf,lo,hi,qP,qQ,tmp)
                if v < best_val - 1e-12:
                    best_val=v; best_t=t; best_pair=(i,j,d)
            if best_pair is not None and best_val < max_corr - 1e-10:
                i,j,d=best_pair
                h2=h.copy()
                h2[i]=np.clip(h2[i]+best_t,0,1); h2[j]=np.clip(h2[j]-best_t,0,1)
                # exact sum? pair preserves but clip could change if t boundary; if sum changed, quick project?
                if abs(float(h2.sum()) - 0.5*n) > 1e-12:
                    h2=project_feasible(h2,n)
                c2=c5_objective(h2,n)
                if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                    h=h2; best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy()
                    recompute corr/C/max_corr
                    no_improve=0; batch_improved=True
                    break
            if not batch_improved and batch == batch_limit-1:
                no_improve +=1
        if no_improve >= 2 or (light and no_improve>=1):
            # perturb working h
            if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
            perturb_count += 1
            scale = perturb_scale / (1.0 + 0.25*(perturb_count-1))
            if scale < 1e-5: scale=1e-5
            h2=h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
            h2=project_feasible(h2,n)
            # ensure bounded
            h=h2
            recompute corr/C
            no_improve=0
            # maybe reduce candidate target
    # final ensure best_h exact project and c
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=c5_objective(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```
Need recompute corr/C after any h change: use helper? Can't closure. Write inline or top-level `state_refresh(h,n,maps)` returns corr,C,max_corr.
```
def refresh_state(h,n,maps):
    corr=compute_corr_pq(h,n); C=compute_coeff_matrix(h,n,maps); return corr,C,float(corr.max())
```
Good.
In optimize, if best_c not improved but working h2 exact c maybe slightly > best_c; still update working? We only update h if exact improved over best_c. But current h may be best_h? We should maintain h as working, possibly worse than best. For local search, if a move improves over current working but not global best? In code above, we update h only if c2 < best_c. But what if best_c is previous and current h=best, any improvement necessary to be working. Since we usually keep h=best unless perturb. If we perturb h to worse, then we should accept moves that improve over current h (even if not global best?) To eventually return best, if working h worse, a move might be better than working but still worse than best; not useful. But to escape, maybe want to climb? We only do exploratory perturb and then if it doesn't improve, best saved. It can accept moves that improve working even if worse than best if we also want to explore? Simpler: keep `current_c` for working h; accept if c2 < current_c - tol; update h but only update best if c2 < best_c. This allows moving out of perturbation maybe back. Better.
Modify:
```
current_c = c5(h)
...
if new gradient c2 < current_c - tol:
   h=newh; current_c=c2; if c2<best_c: update best
...
pair: if c2 < current_c - tol: h=h2; current_c=c2; if c2<best_c update best; recompute; no_improve=0; break
else if best_pair but c2 not < current_c: no_improve...
```
If best_c improves but current_c was lower than best? Actually current_c can be >best after perturb; accept moves reducing current_c. Good.
Need when recompute corr/C after h change, `max_corr = float(corr.max())`; keep current_c as exact c5? They may differ tiny; okay.

In gradient step, use `current_c` to compare. It returns h if exact c2 < current_c. Good.

In pair line search, `best_val` from our corr may be lower than max_corr, but exact c2 may not improve current_c. If not, we still can try next batch? If candidate line search based on stale exact mismatch, maybe no. We'll increment no_improve.

Perturb: We can use random noise scale relative to n? h values O0.5; scale 0.03. Project. It may greatly worsen; but best saved. If no time, returns best.

Need avoid too many C recomputes. In optimize, after gradient/pair/perturb, call `refresh_state`. In pair batch if no accept and no_improve reaches threshold, perturb. Good.

Set `gradient_attempts`: Maybe run gradient step every loop until it fails. Each loop tries once; if fails, then pair. Pair if fails several batches, perturb. After perturb, gradient again. Good.

Light mode: candidate_count 60, batch_limit 1, gradients fewer, perturb threshold 1. For resampled n large, less time.

Resampling function:
```
def interpolate_h(h,n_new):
    n=len(h)
    if n==n_new: return h.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return h_new
```
Need if step function left endpoints; okay.

Run phases:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); dead=t0+budget_s-10
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
    except:
       x=np.linspace(0,2,250,endpoint=False); h0=0.5+0.2*np.cos(np.pi*x)+... ensure
    n0=len(h0)
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=c5_objective(h0,n0)
    # Phase 1 optimize original for 450s (or if parent not exact)
    d1=min(t0+450, dead)
    if dead - t0 > 120:
       h1,c1=optimize_h(best_h,n0,d1,rng,light=False)
       if c1 < best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
    # Phase 2 resample and optimize
    n_options=[]
    for nn in [256, 320, 384, 512]:
       if nn != len(best_h) and nn > 0:
          n_options.append(nn)
    for nn in n_options:
       rem=dead-time.time()
       if rem < 120: break
       alloc=min(180, rem*0.4)
       h_new=insert... project
       # quick eval; if already better than best? maybe
       c_new=c5_objective(h_new,nn)
       h_opt,c_opt=optimize_h(h_new,nn,time.time()+alloc,rng,light=True)
       if c_opt < best_c: best_h,best_c=h_opt,c_opt
    # Phase 3 final polish best
    rem=dead-time.time()
    if rem > 50:
       h_opt,c_opt=optimize_h(best_h,len(best_h),time.time()+min(rem-5,60),rng,light=True)
       if c_opt < best_c: update
    # maybe if time and parent not used? no
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h)); best_c=c5_objective(best_h,len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)
```

Hmm.

- Outer loop up to many iterations.
- If `do_gradient` True (every 1 or after perturb), attempt gradient; if no improvement, set do_gradient=False.
- Then run pair batches. If pair no improve and no_improve threshold, perturb and set do_gradient=True.
This saves.

Let's think if `compute_coeff_matrix` derivative should include linear sums `a.sum()` in P/Q? Derivative of a.sum() wrt h_u is 1 for first variables; derivative of -ab is -h_v for first, -h_u for second. Our formulas match. For P_k first terms index set i=0..m-1: coefficient for node r if r first (r<m? Actually r as i): 1 - h_{r+k}. If r second (r-k in 0..m-1): -h_{r-k}. We used right condition r+k<n -> r<m; left r-k>=0. Good. For Q first: node r as i+k -> r>=k and r-k<m (r<n) -> 1 - h_{r-k}. We used left condition. Q second: r<i+k? r<m -> -h_{r+k}; right condition. Good.

Let's refine optimizer code in detail to avoid variable mishaps.

```
def optimize_h(start_h, n, deadline, rng, light=False):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(start_h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    current_c = c5_objective(h, n)
    best_c = current_c
    maps = make_index_maps(n)
    M = 2*n - 1
    Lbuf = np.empty(M, dtype=float)
    tmp = np.empty(M, dtype=float)
    corr, C, max_corr = refresh_state(h, n, maps)
    no_improve = 0
    do_gradient = True
    if light:
       batch_limit=1; ccount=70; temps=(1.0,2.0); amps=(0.005,0.01,0.02)
       grad_interval=2? no
    else:
       batch_limit=3; ccount=160; temps=(0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0); amps=(0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05)
    perturb_count=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
        # gradient
        if do_gradient and time.time() < deadline - 15:
            newh = gradient_step(h, n, corr, C, current_c, temps, amps)
            if newh is not None:
                h = newh
                current_c = c5_objective(h,n)
                if current_c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = current_c; best_h = h.copy()
                corr, C, max_corr = refresh_state(h,n,maps)
                no_improve = 0
                do_gradient = False
                continue
            do_gradient = False
        # pair batches
        improved=False
        for batch in range(batch_limit):
            if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
            cands = generate_candidates(n,h,C,corr,rng,ccount)
            if len(cands)==0: break
            best_val = max_corr + 1e-9
            best_pair=None; best_t=0.0
            for (i,j) in cands:
                if time.time() > deadline - 6: break
                lo=-float(h[i]); hi=1.0-float(h[j])
                if hi - lo < 1e-12: continue
                d=abs(i-j)
                qP=2*d-1; qQ=qP+1
                np.subtract(C[i], C[j], out=Lbuf)
                t,v = minimize_transfer(corr, Lbuf, lo, hi, qP, qQ, tmp)
                if v < best_val - 1e-12:
                    best_val=v; best_t=t; best_pair=(i,j,d)
            if best_pair is not None:
                i,j,d=best_pair
                h2=h.copy()
                h2[i] = float(np.clip(h2[i] + best_t, 0.0, 1.0))
                h2[j] = float(np.clip(h2[j] - best_t, 0.0, 1.0))
                # if clip altered sum, project quickly
                if abs(float(h2.sum()) - 0.5*n) > 1e-12:
                    h2 = project_feasible(h2,n)
                c2 = c5_objective(h2,n)
                if c2 < current_c - 1e-13:
                    h = h2
                    current_c = c2
                    if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = c2; best_h = h.copy()
                    corr, C, max_corr = refresh_state(h,n,maps)
                    no_improve = 0
                    improved=True
                    break
        if improved:
            continue
        no_improve += 1
        if no_improve >= (2 if not light else 1):
            if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
            perturb_count += 1
            scale = 0.02 / (1.0 + 0.2*(perturb_count-1))
            if scale < 1e-4: scale = 1e-4
            h2 = h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
            h2 = project_feasible(h2,n)
            # If perturbation got worse than best, allow but maybe stop if too worse?
            current_c = c5_objective(h2,n)
            h = h2
            corr,C,max_corr = refresh_state(h,n,maps)
            no_improve=0
            do_gradient=True
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = c5_objective(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c
```

Now, one subtle mathematical issue: Our pair line search uses `corr` P/Q but exact c5 from np.correlate. Are we sure `np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')` values are exactly P/Q and not also includes lags with reversed order? Yes set. But what about the sign of lags? Our corr P/Q order max same. Updates based on P/Q definitions. If np.correlate computes `P` and `Q` but swapped positions, max unaffected. Good.

Now, maybe exact c5 includes k=0 where h(1-h); for pair update, self q=-2 and L=2(h_j-h_i). Let's verify for pair +i -j, t in interval. Old self sum includes a(1-a)+b(1-b). New: (a+t)(1-a-t)+(b-t)(1-b+t). Expand: a(1-a)+t(1-2a)-t^2 + b(1-b)+t(2b-1)-t^2 = old +2t(b-a)-2t^2. L0 from C_i-C_j=1-2a - (1-2b)=2b-2a. q0=-2. Good.

    n=len(h)
    if n==n_new: return h.copy()
    x_old=np.arange(n)* (2.0/n)

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    for _ in range(5):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            return h
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
            if room <= 1e-15:
                break
            h[idx] += diff if diff < room else room
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            mass = h[idx]
            if mass <= 1e-15:
                break
            h[idx] += diff if diff > -mass else -mass

    for _ in range(1000):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h

    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    for _ in range(10):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
            if room <= 1e-15:
                break
            h[idx] += diff if diff < room else room
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            mass = h[idx]
            if mass <= 1e-15:
                break
            h[idx] += diff if diff > -mass else -mass

    return h


def c5_objective(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)


def compute_corr_pq(h, n):
    m_total = 2 * n - 1
    corr = np.empty(m_total, dtype=float)
    sh = float(h.sum())
    corr[0] = sh - float(np.dot(h, h))
    for k in range(1, n):
        m = n - k
        a = h[:m]
        b = h[k:k + m]
        ab = float(np.dot(a, b))
        corr[2 * k - 1] = float(a.sum()) - ab
        corr[2 * k] = float(b.sum()) - ab
    return corr


def make_index_maps(n):
    empty = np.empty(0, dtype=np.intp)
    pl = [None] * n
    ql = [None] * n
    pr = [None] * n
    qr = [None] * n
    for r in range(n):
        left_len = r
        if left_len > 0:
            arr = 2 * np.arange(1, left_len + 1, dtype=np.intp) - 1
            pl[r] = arr
            ql[r] = arr + 1
        else:
            pl[r] = empty
            ql[r] = empty

        right_len = n - r - 1
        if right_len > 0:
            arr = 2 * np.arange(1, right_len + 1, dtype=np.intp) - 1
            pr[r] = arr
            qr[r] = arr + 1
        else:
            pr[r] = empty
            qr[r] = empty
    return pl, ql, pr, qr


def compute_coeff_matrix(h, n, maps):
    pl, ql, pr, qr = maps
    m_total = 2 * n - 1
    C = np.zeros((n, m_total), dtype=float)
    for r in range(n):
        cr = C[r]
        cr[0] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h[r]

        idx = pl[r]
        if idx.size:
            left = h[r - 1::-1]
            cr[idx] -= left
            cr[ql[r]] += 1.0 - left

        idx = pr[r]
        if idx.size:
            right = h[r + 1:]
            cr[idx] += 1.0 - right
            cr[qr[r]] -= right
    return C


def refresh_state(h, n, maps):
    corr = compute_corr_pq(h, n)
    C = compute_coeff_matrix(h, n, maps)
    return corr, C, float(corr.max())


def transfer_value(corr, L, t, qP, qQ, tmp):
    np.multiply(L, t, out=tmp)
    tmp += corr
    t2 = t * t
    tmp[0] -= 2.0 * t2
    tmp[qP] += t2
    tmp[qQ] += t2
    return float(tmp.max())


def minimize_transfer(corr, L, lo, hi, qP, qQ, tmp):
    if hi < lo:
        lo, hi = hi, lo
    span = hi - lo
    if span < 1e-14:
        v0 = transfer_value(corr, L, 0.0, qP, qQ, tmp)
        return 0.0, v0

    best_t = 0.0
    best_v = transfer_value(corr, L, 0.0, qP, qQ, tmp)

    v_lo = transfer_value(corr, L, lo, qP, qQ, tmp)
    if v_lo < best_v:
        best_v = v_lo
        best_t = lo

    v_hi = transfer_value(corr, L, hi, qP, qQ, tmp)
    if v_hi < best_v:
        best_v = v_hi
        best_t = hi

    if span > 1e-5:
        NG = 12
        step = span / float(NG + 1)
        grid_t = [lo]
        grid_v = [v_lo]
        for a in range(1, NG + 2):
            t = lo + a * step
            v = transfer_value(corr, L, t, qP, qQ, tmp)
            grid_t.append(t)
            grid_v.append(v)
            if v < best_v:
                best_v = v
                best_t = t

        idx = int(np.argmin(grid_v))
        left_idx = max(0, idx - 1)
        right_idx = min(len(grid_t) - 1, idx + 1)
        left = grid_t[left_idx]
        right = grid_t[right_idx]
    else:
        left = lo
        right = hi

    if right - left > 1e-12:
        iters = 18 if span > 0.05 else 10
        for _ in range(iters):
            m1 = left + (right - left) / 3.0
            m2 = right - (right - left) / 3.0
            v1 = transfer_value(corr, L, m1, qP, qQ, tmp)
            v2 = transfer_value(corr, L, m2, qP, qQ, tmp)
            if v1 <= v2:
                right = m2
            else:
                left = m1
        for tt in (left, right, 0.5 * (left + right)):
            v = transfer_value(corr, L, tt, qP, qQ, tmp)
            if v < best_v:
                best_v = v
                best_t = tt

    return best_t, best_v


def generate_candidates(n, h, C, corr, rng, max_candidates):
    cands = []
    seen = set()
    m_total = len(corr)
    maxc = float(corr.max())

    top_k = min(10, m_total)
    if top_k >= m_total:
        top_lags = np.arange(m_total, dtype=int)
    else:
        top_lags = np.argpartition(corr, -top_k)[-top_k:].copy()
    order_c = np.argsort(corr[top_lags])[::-1]
    top_lags = top_lags[order_c]

    for s in top_lags:
        order = np.argsort(C[:, s])
        side = min(3, n)
        for ia in range(side):
            for ib in range(side):
                i = int(order[n - 1 - ia])
                j = int(order[ib])
                if i == j:
                    continue
                if i > j:
                    i, j = j, i
                key = i * n + j
                if key not in seen and len(cands) < max_candidates:
                    seen.add(key)
                    cands.append((i, j))

        for _ in range(2):
            ai = int(rng.randint(0, min(5, n)))
            bi = int(rng.randint(0, min(5, n)))
            i = int(order[n - 1 - ai])
            j = int(order[bi])
            if i == j:
                continue
            if i > j:
                i, j = j, i
            key = i * n + j
            if key not in seen and len(cands) < max_candidates:
                seen.add(key)
                cands.append((i, j))

    w = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for s in top_lags:
        wt = float(np.exp((corr[s] - maxc) / 1.0))
        if wt > 0.0:
            w += C[:, s] * wt

    if float(np.max(np.abs(w))) > 1e-14:
        wo = np.argsort(w)
        side = min(3, n)
        for ia in range(side):
            for ib in range(side):
                i = int(wo[n - 1 - ia])
                j = int(wo[ib])
                if i == j:
                    continue
                if i > j:
                    i, j = j, i
                key = i * n + j
                if key not in seen and len(cands) < max_candidates:
                    seen.add(key)
                    cands.append((i, j))

    hi_order = np.argsort(h)[::-1][:min(5, n)]
    lo_order = np.argsort(h)[:min(5, n)]
    for i in hi_order:
        for j in lo_order:
            i = int(i)
            j = int(j)
            if i == j:
                continue
            if i > j:
                i, j = j, i
            key = i * n + j
            if key not in seen and len(cands) < max_candidates:
                seen.add(key)
                cands.append((i, j))

    attempts = 0
    while len(cands) < max_candidates and attempts < 8 * max_candidates:
        attempts += 1
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        if i > j:
            i, j = j, i
        key = i * n + j
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            cands.append((i, j))

    return cands[:max_candidates]


def gradient_step(h, n, corr, C, current_c, temps, amps):
    maxc = float(corr.max())
    best_h = None
    best_c = current_c

    for temp in temps:
        diff = (corr - maxc) / float(temp)
        w = np.exp(diff)
        tot = float(w.sum())
        if tot <= 1e-15:
            continue
        w /= tot

        g = C.T @ w
        g -= float(g.mean())
        gm = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gm < 1e-13:
            continue
        g /= gm

        for amp in amps:
            h2 = h - amp * g
            np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0, out=h2)
            h2 = project_feasible(h2, n)
            c2 = c5_objective(h2, n)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()

    return best_h


def optimize_h(start_h, n, deadline, rng, light=False):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(start_h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    current_c = c5_objective(h, n)
    best_c = current_c

    if light:
        batch_limit = 1
        candidate_count = 70
        temps = (1.0, 2.0)
        amps = (0.005, 0.01, 0.02)
        perturb_reset = 1
    else:
        batch_limit = 3
        candidate_count = 180
        temps = (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0)
        amps = (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05)
        perturb_reset = 2

    maps = make_index_maps(n)
    m_total = 2 * n - 1
    Lbuf = np.empty(m_total, dtype=float)
    tmp = np.empty(m_total, dtype=float)

    corr, C, max_corr = refresh_state(h, n, maps)
    no_improve = 0
    do_gradient = True
    perturb_count = 0

    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
        if do_gradient and time.time() < deadline - 20:
            newh = gradient_step(h, n, corr, C, current_c, temps, amps)
            do_gradient = False
            if newh is not None:
                h = newh
                current_c = c5_objective(h, n)
                if current_c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = current_c
                    best_h = h.copy()
                corr, C, max_corr = refresh_state(h, n, maps)
                no_improve = 0
                continue

        improved = False
        for _ in range(batch_limit):
            if time.time() > deadline - 5:
                break

            cands = generate_candidates(n, h, C, corr, rng, candidate_count)
            if not cands:
                break

            best_val = max_corr + 1e-9
            best_pair = None
            best_t = 0.0

            for i, j in cands:
                if time.time() > deadline - 6:
                    break
                lo = -float(h[i])
                hi = 1.0 - float(h[j])
                if hi - lo < 1e-12:
                    continue
                d = abs(i - j)
                qP = 2 * d - 1
                qQ = qP + 1
                np.subtract(C[i], C[j], out=Lbuf)
                t, v = minimize_transfer(corr, Lbuf, lo, hi, qP, qQ, tmp)
                if v < best_val - 1e-12:
                    best_val = v
                    best_t = t
                    best_pair = (i, j, d)

            if best_pair is not None:
                i, j, d = best_pair
                h2 = h.copy()
                h2[i] = float(np.clip(h2[i] + best_t, 0.0, 1.0))
                h2[j] = float(np.clip(h2[j] - best_t, 0.0, 1.0))
                if abs(float(h2.sum()) - 0.5 * n) > 1e-12:
                    h2 = project_feasible(h2, n)

                c2 = c5_objective(h2, n)
                if c2 < current_c - 1e-13:
                    h = h2
                    current_c = c2
                    if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = c2
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    corr, C, max_corr = refresh_state(h, n, maps)
                    no_improve = 0
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            continue

        no_improve += 1
        if no_improve >= perturb_reset:
            if time.time() > deadline - 10:
                break
            perturb_count += 1
            scale = 0.02 / (1.0 + 0.25 * float(perturb_count - 1))
            if scale < 1e-4:
                scale = 1e-4
            base = h if (best_c - current_c < 1e-10) else best_h
            h2 = base + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
            h2 = project_feasible(h2, n)
            h = h2
            current_c = c5_objective(h, n)
            corr, C, max_corr = refresh_state(h, n, maps)
            no_improve = 0
            do_gradient = True

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = c5_objective(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c


def interpolate_h(h, n_new):
    n = len(h)
    if n == n_new:
        return h.copy()
    x_old = np.arange(n, dtype=float) * (2.0 / float(n))
    x_new = np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) * (2.0 / float(n_new))
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)


def fallback_h(n, rng):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = 0.5
    h += 0.18 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    h += 0.08 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    h += 0.05 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    h += rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float, copy=True)
        n0 = len(h0)
        if n0 <= 0:
            raise ValueError("empty parent")
        h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    except Exception:
        n0 = 250
        h0 = fallback_h(n0, rng)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = c5_objective(best_h, len(best_h))

    if deadline - t0 > 150:
        alloc1 = min(430.0, deadline - t0 - 100.0)
        h1, c1 = optimize_h(best_h, len(best_h), t0 + alloc1, rng, light=False)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1

    n_options = []
    base_n = len(best_h)
    for nn in (256, 320, 384, 480, 640):
        if nn <= 8 or abs(nn - base_n) <= 8:
            continue
        if nn not in n_options:
            n_options.append(nn)
        if len(n_options) >= 3:
            break

    for nn in n_options:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 140.0:
            break
        alloc = min(170.0, max(60.0, rem * 0.30))
        if alloc < 60.0:
            break
        h_new = interpolate_h(best_h, nn)
        h_new = project_feasible(h_new, nn)
        h_opt, c_opt = optimize_h(h_new, nn, time.time() + alloc, rng, light=True)
        if c_opt < best_c:
            best_c = c_opt
            best_h = h_opt

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 55.0:
        final_alloc = min(rem - 8.0, 80.0)
        if final_alloc > 30.0:
            h_opt, c_opt = optimize_h(best_h, len(best_h), time.time() + final_alloc, rng, light=True)
            if c_opt < best_c:
                best_c = c_opt
                best_h = h_opt

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = c5_objective(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, int(len(best_h))
```
